[2026-08-08 21:25] Cristian [DL], ↪(已验证 APP APP @Sapphire Primal Balance (PB), also known as Druid's challenge rewar): Primal Balance (PB), also known as Druid's challenge reward (DDCR), allows the Grand Balance  (GB) spell to target more buildings based on mana produced in this Reincarnation.  PB+# denotes how many extra targets PB gives you, from PB+1 (1 extra target, GB targets the 2 weakest buildings) up to PB+10 (GB targets all 11 buildings). PB can go beyond +10, but it gives GB no extra target, and the excess amount instead provide a small production bonus to all buildings. If a build requires PB+X, it means you must produce enough mana (in this Reincarnation) until PB gives that many extra targets. https://files.catbox.moe/t2dtcl.png

And you have to have 400 REs of that faction's faction coin affiliations. Lineage levels stay across R's
